([sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar](https://github.com/sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-publish-council) · ★4)
- **radar-publish-notion**: Publishes the run to Notion. Upserts one row per competitor in the Competitors database (matched by domain, with a Change field for week-over-week tracking) and creates a Briefings page holding the full briefing. Use after radar-merge-briefing. Requires the databases to exist (radar-provision-notion). Writing rows to an approved database is autonomous. ([sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar](https://github.com/sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-publish-notion) · ★4)
- **radar-pulse**: Iterate the FULL social/community + trusted-curator source lists in SOURCES.md (Reddit, Hacker News, YouTube curators, explainer/digest pointer blogs, Hugging Face, best-effort X/Instagram) on every daily run — every listed entry, fetched by its recorded access method, NOT a sampled subset. Social/curator is an INTAKE LANE ONLY — it can feed observation_queue (unverified) and a community-pulse note, but NEVER becomes trend evidence. Use after the lab sweep. ([Neetx/ai-security-research-radar](https://github.com/Neetx/ai-security-research-radar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/radar-pulse) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **radar-pulse-compression-reference-fixtures**: Apply deterministic engineering checks to compare pulse-compression output against bounded synthetic reference vectors to catch correlation, scaling, and latency regressions. ([Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code](https://github.com/Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code/tree/HEAD/curated-skill-sources/aeris-radar-engineering/radar-pulse-compression-reference-fixtures) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **radar-pwa-install**: Install DUP Market Radar dashboard as a phone/desktop PWA panel over HTTPS or localhost. ([jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill](https://github.com/jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill/tree/HEAD/skills/dev-tools/gruver87-radar-pwa-install) · ★81)

- **radar-reconstruction-filter-response**: Apply deterministic engineering checks to check the DAC reconstruction filter simulation against declared passband, stopband, and phase-response requirements. - **radar-regulatorio**: Esta skill deve ser usada para produzir o radar regulatório da empresa: "radar regulatório", "o que mudou na legislação", "novidades de compliance", "prazos regulatórios", ou quando uma tarefa agendada pedir a rotina "radar-regulatorio". Acompanha leis, normas, decisões e políticas de plataforma que afetam a empresa, separa o que vale do que é proposta e mantém a tabela de prazos. ([LeandroMarianoNunes/claude-agents-framework](https://github.com/LeandroMarianoNunes/claude-agents-framework/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-regulatorio))
- **radar-render-dashboard**: Regenerate README.md, the repo's landing page and only output surface: badges, a 3-4 bullet "since last scan" digest with inline links, a clickable trend table, the study shelf, and an output map. Use after every change to TRENDS.md, in the same commit. README.md is fully derived and never edited by hand: if it disagrees with TRENDS.md, TRENDS.md wins — regenerate. On daily runs it also overwrites SUMMARY.md — the run's results in at most 6 one-line linked bullets, no process detail. ([Neetx/ai-security-research-radar](https://github.com/Neetx/ai-security-research-radar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/radar-render-dashboard) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **radar-render-dashboard**: Renders the self-contained local HTML dashboard for a run by injecting the run's data JSON into the dashboard template. Produces one HTML file with inline CSS and JS and no external requests that the user can open directly in a browser. Use after radar-merge-briefing, as the last surface in a /radar run. ([sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar](https://github.com/sabahudin-web/competitive-intelligence-radar/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-render-dashboard) · ★4)
- **radar-replay-csv-schema-validation**: Apply deterministic engineering checks to validate recorded/replay CSV schemas before plotting or comparing signals, including unit and provenance metadata. ([Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code](https://github.com/Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code/tree/HEAD/curated-skill-sources/aeris-radar-engineering/radar-replay-csv-schema-validation) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **radar-repo-watch**: Watch GitHub every daily run for behind-the-scenes movement — across watched REPOS (releases, merged PRs, hot issues), watched PROFILES/USERS (what a key author ships next), and FORK TREES (notable forks up to depth 3, scored by a notability metric). Releases and merges are citable artifacts; profile/issue/fork movement is a queue signal until it lands. Default to GitHub's public Atom feeds + Tavily; the REST API is optional. Use during the daily scan, after the lab sweep. Source lists live in SOURCES.md → "GitHub watch". ([Neetx/ai-security-research-radar](https://github.com/Neetx/ai-security-research-radar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/radar-repo-watch) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **radar-report**: Aggregate research-corpus radar sidecars into a corpus or per-cluster freshness report — totals, overdue count, per-cluster / per-GRADE / per-trajectory breakdowns, an overdue table, and per-radar rationale snippets. Runs via `aiwg corpus radar-report`. ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/tree/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/research-complete/skills/radar-report) · ★230 · 2 copies)
- **radar-review**: Reviews Session Radar exports (session-radar-*.json) together with this project's Claude Code configuration (CLAUDE.md, settings permissions, .mcp.json, skills, agents) and proposes concrete configuration changes, each with its evidence and the radar metric that will confirm it. Uses the tokens of the current session. Run it only when the person asks for it, for example with /session-radar:radar-review, optionally followed by the path of an export. ([NeuralSeam/ns-session-radar](https://github.com/NeuralSeam/ns-session-radar/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-review) · ★4)
- **radar-review**: Review your radar catalogue in a local web UI — browse, star, dismiss, annotate, and filter discovered tools and insights. ([flippyhead/radar](https://github.com/flippyhead/radar/tree/HEAD/plugins/radar/skills/radar-review) · ★3)

Trigger on requests such as "add this to the second brain", "what do we know about X", "check the second brain". - **radar-self-eval**: Compute the radar's self-calibration: weekly funnel metrics (queue dynamics, exploration compliance, off-axis rate), a monthly hit/miss retrospective against what actually became big, and up to 3 curator proposals. Use during every weekly recalibration, after the source-strategy review; results go into the append-only `logs/calibration.md` and into the weekly report. Reads or builds the instance config, publishes the dashboard artifact, fills its database from Linear and creates the daily brief scheduled task. - **radar-signal-processing**: Parse raw radar I/Q captures (CW or FMCW mmWave) and produce a cleaned 1-D slow-time signal ready for motion or vital-signs analysis. Use when Claude needs to read interleaved I/Q binary, do a Range FFT on FMCW chirps, remove static clutter, pick a subject range bin, extract phase with unwrapping, or debug why a radar pipeline is returning garbage. Not for pulse/UWB range gating, MIMO beamforming, or Doppler-only gesture radar. ([xuansenpa1/skillrevise](https://github.com/xuansenpa1/skillrevise/tree/HEAD/data/skillsbench/tasks/radar-vital-signs/environment/skills/radar-signal-processing) · ★58)
- **radar-signal-processing**: Turns raw CW/FMCW mmWave I/Q captures into a cleaned 1-D slow-time phase trace: Range FFT, clutter removal, range-bin pick, unwrap, then FIR decimate before sub-Hz filters. Use when parsing radar I/Q, vital-signs/breathing phase, or debugging a garbage radar pipeline. Not for pulse/UWB time-of-flight, MIMO angle-of-arrival, or micro-Doppler gesture spectrograms. Never use scipy decimate IIR (phase distortion) or unwrap undersampled jumps greater than pi. ([Kayforkind/skill-slice](https://github.com/Kayforkind/skill-slice/tree/HEAD/skills/radar-signal-processing))
